# CB99 accessibility identifier registry

Base is `origin/rc/20261108` at `2f6d5041a57d876d3b0a953f56ecf5208089622f`.
The comparison tree is `origin/ui/d6` at `b85ae2741cba5afe0a1320097833e69cb9eb23a0`. That commit was read and was not merged.
`ui/d7` was not fetched and is not a source for this registry.

## Counts

| Set | Count |
| --- | ---: |
| Literal identifiers on this base | 6162 |
| Literal identifiers on ui/d6 | 6146 |
| On both | 6139 |
| Only on this base | 23 |
| Only on ui/d6, not in this branch | 7 |
| Interpolation templates on this base | 779 |
| Interpolation templates on ui/d6 | 780 |
| Interpolation templates only on ui/d6, not in this branch | 1 |
| Identifiers added in this branch | 0 |
| Identifiers only documented | 6949 |

No identifier was added. Every literal a test already expects from non-view logic on this base is already published by that logic. The ui/d6-only values below are view accessibility identifiers. They were not copied onto this branch, and no D6 view was restyled.

No test was deleted. `tools/current_ci_manifest.json` was not refreshed. This registry was produced by reading Swift sources. Xcode was not run.

## What is counted

A literal is an accessibility identifier when production Swift under `App/` or `Core/` does any of the following:

- Passes it to `.accessibilityIdentifier`, including a string built by concatenating two literals or a literal prefix with the branches of a ternary.
- Returns it from a property named `accessibilityID` or `accessibilityIdentifier`.
- Passes it to a parameter or stored property that the same type applies with `.accessibilityIdentifier`, `.cyIdentifier`, or `.cyOptionalAccessibilityIdentifier`.

An interpolation template is the source text of an `.accessibilityIdentifier` argument that contains `\(`. Tests are not a source. The same spelling on a localization key, a search prompt, or a navigation title is not an accessibility identifier.

`roam.search` is a search prompt on this base (`App/RoamBrowserView.swift`). ui/d6 also applies it as `.accessibilityIdentifier("roam.search")`. Only the accessibility identifier is marked not in this branch.

## Non-view identifiers

Non-view logic is `Core/`. These identifiers are published by Core types on this base. ui/d6 does not add or remove any of them. No Core test expects a further accessibility identifier that Core does not already return.

- `roam.home.recovery.needsCheck` in `Core/RoamPassportHome.swift`
- `roam.home.recovery.recovered` in `Core/RoamPassportHome.swift`

- `roam.receivedStamp.tab.received` in `Core/RoamReceivedStampPresentation.swift`
